TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于体素的三维地震相分割基准，利用公开数据集（F3和Parihaka）并采用标准化数据划分与评估指标，比较了三类现代3D分割架构，建立了强基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震相分割研究大多将原始3D地震体预处理为2D切片（如沿inline和crossline方向），将其视为纯2D分割任务，这会导致切片间不连续，无法保留3D数据的空间与结构连续性，限制了模型学习连贯地质模式的能力。因此需要一种基于体素的3D分割方法及相应基准。

Method: 构建了一个可复现的基于体素的3D地震相分割基准，基于公开的Netherlands F3和Parihaka地震数据体，采用标准化的数据划分和评估指标，并评估了三类具有代表性的现代3D分割架构。

Result: 通过评估三类代表性3D分割架构，建立了强基线结果，揭示了该领域的潜力和尚存挑战。

Conclusion: 该基准为3D地震相分割提供了标准化和可复现的评估平台，为未来研究指明了方向，同时凸显了从2D切片方法转向3D体素方法的重要性。

Abstract: Seismic facies segmentation has emerged as a significant challenge in geophysics, requiring robust methods and systems to effectively identify geologically analogous facies with limited labeled data. Although existing studies have shown promising results in 2D facies segmentation, they often preprocess the original 3D seismic volumes into sets of 2D slices, typically the inline and crossline directions, and treat this problem as a purely 2D segmentation task. This simplification introduces discontinuities across slices and fails to preserve the spatial and structural continuity in 3D seismic data, thus limiting the model's ability to learn coherent geological patterns. In this work, we present a comparative and reproducible benchmark for voxel-based 3D seismic facies segmentation, built upon publicly available seismic volumes including the Netherlands F3 and the Parihaka datasets, with standardized data splits and evaluation metrics. By evaluating the three representative families of modern 3D segmentation architectures, we establish strong baseline results that highlight the potential and remaining challenges for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [34] [InstructVVT: Instruction-Driven Video Virtual Try-On without Auxiliary Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14070)
*Dingbao Shao,Song Wu,Xinyu Chen,Qian Wang,Jiahang Li,Kuai Jiang,Jiang Lin,Yuhang Liu,Ziyu Chen,Duo Li,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ziheng Tang,Rongrong Liu,Yanlun Peng,Liang Li,Junlan Feng,Lujia Jin,Ting Zhang,Jian Yang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstructVVT是一个基于DiT的视频虚拟试穿框架，通过指令驱动和参考引导实现无推理时空间先验的衣物替换，采用双层级参考条件方案和人类偏好对齐优化，在ViViD-S和TripVVT-Bench上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖手工设计的空间先验（如掩码、姿态）进行编辑控制，这些先验在非约束的真实世界视频中容易失效，且会将丰富的视觉上下文压缩为不完整的结构信号。此外，标准的重建目标无法充分捕捉试穿场景特有的人类偏好。

Method: InstructVVT基于Diffusion Transformer（DiT），无需推理时空间先验。核心在于双层级参考条件方案：1）MLLM从输入三元组（源视频、参考服装、指令）推断语义编辑token，用于目标消歧和结构保持；2）轻量级条件路径显式注入细粒度视觉服装细节。此外，设计了试穿特有的奖励函数，并利用DiffusionNFT算法使模型与人类偏好对齐。

Result: 在ViViD-S和TripVVT-Bench上的大量实验表明，InstructVVT在服装保真度、结构保持和时间一致性方面均优于最先进的开源方法，同时需要的推理时控制更少。

Conclusion: InstructVVT通过从输入三元组直接恢复细粒度控制，消除了对推理时手工空间先验的依赖，证明了指令驱动加双层级参考条件加人类偏好对齐的安全高效范式在视频虚拟试穿任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on is a highly constrained editing task requiring the precise replacement of a target person's clothing while strictly preserving the original video's spatial structure and temporal dynamics. Existing methods heavily rely on auxiliary handcrafted spatial priors (e.g., masks, poses) for editing control. However, these priors are prone to failure in unconstrained real-world videos and often compress rich visual context into incomplete structural signals. Furthermore, standard reconstruction objectives fail to fully capture try-on-specific human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose InstructVVT, an instruction-driven and reference-guided video virtual try-on framework based on a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates without inference-time spatial priors. Our core insight is to recover fine-grained control directly from the input triplet (source video, reference garment, and instruction) via a dual-level reference conditioning scheme. Specifically, an MLLM infers semantic edit tokens for target disambiguation and structural preservation, while a lightweight conditioning pathway explicitly injects fine-grained visual garment details. Finally, we design a try-on-specific reward and utilize the DiffusionNFT algorithm to align the model with human preferences. Extensive experiments on ViViD-S and TripVVT-Bench demonstrate that InstructVVT outperforms state-of-the-art open-source methods in garment fidelity, structural preservation, and temporal consistency, despite requiring fewer inference-time controls.

</details>


### [35] [Owner3D: Ownership-Guided Style Writing for Training-Free Localized 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14078)
*Suchang Tao,Kaifeng Shi,Zhiyan Liu,Zhuoyuan Jiang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Owner3D是一个面向大规模重建模型(LRMs)的免训练局部3D风格化框架，通过所有权引导的风格写入将参考风格限制于目标区域，引入边界双槽解决语义边界处的风格泄漏与外观歧义，并使用表面优先的纹理读出机制结合表面、3D和triplane所有权证据恢复外观。在GSO和PartNet构建的基准上，相比StyleSplat和LAENeRF分别将外观泄漏降低86.4%和89.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部3D风格化旨在修改指定物体部分的外观而保留其余表面。在大型重建模型(LRMs)中，风格被注入到渲染前的中间外观表示中，但紧凑的triplane特征在目标与非目标表面间共享，导致风格泄漏和边界模糊问题，难以实现精确的局部样式控制。

Method: 1) 所有权引导的风格写入(ownership-guided style writing)：将参考风格注入限制在目标区域，产生单一局部风格化triplane，无需额外训练且避免全局风格与外观的分离表示。2) 边界双槽(boundary dual slots)：为目标和非目标区域维护分离的局部特征源，解决语义边界附近的外观歧义。3) 表面优先纹理读出(surface-first texture readout)：分层组合表面、3D和triplane三层所有权证据，在不完整可见性下稳健恢复外观。

Result: 在由Google Scanned Objects和PartNet构建的基准实验中，Owner3D在目标区域风格保真度和非目标区域外观保持方面一致优于现有3D风格化方法，相比StyleSplat外观泄漏降低86.4%，相比LAENeRF降低89.9%。

Conclusion: Owner3D通过将局部外观控制直接集成到LRM重建过程中，在不需额外训练的前提下有效解决了局部3D风格化中的风格泄漏与边界歧义问题，显著提升了局部风格化的精度。

Abstract: Localized 3D stylization aims to modify the appearance of a specified object part while preserving the remaining surfaces. In large reconstruction models (LRMs), this task is challenging because style is injected into intermediate appearance representations before rendering, while compact triplane features are shared across target and non-target surfaces, causing style leakage and boundary ambiguity. We propose Owner3D, a training-free framework for localized 3D stylization that integrates localized appearance control directly into the LRM reconstruction process. Specifically, Owner3D introduces ownership-guided style writing to restrict reference-style injection to target regions, producing a single localized stylized triplane without additional training while avoiding separate global style and appearance representations. To resolve appearance ambiguity near semantic boundaries, we further introduce boundary dual slots that maintain separate local feature sources for target and non-target regions. Finally, a surface-first texture readout hierarchically combines surface, 3D, and triplane ownership evidence to robustly recover appearance under incomplete visibility. Experiments on a benchmark constructed from Google Scanned Objects and PartNet demonstrate that Owner3D consistently outperforms existing 3D stylization methods in target-region style fidelity and non-target appearance preservation, reducing appearance leakage by 86.4% and 89.9% compared with StyleSplat and LAENeRF, respectively.

</details>


### [36] [CoDS: Robust Collaborative Perception via Expert-driven Detection and BEV Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14085)
*Jinlong Wang,Yuang Jia,Junhong Lin,Nannan Li,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDS框架，通过协作可靠性图（CoRM）、语义混合专家（S-MoE）和双向任务互补交互（BTCI）三个模块，联合训练检测与BEV分割任务，以提升多智能体协同感知对位姿误差和通信延迟等噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知通过多智能体信息交换突破单视角局限，但位姿误差和通信延迟等多源噪声会降低融合特征质量，制约感知性能。单独检测或分割任务难以充分利用多模态互补信息来抵抗噪声干扰。

Method: 1) 提出Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM)显式量化融合特征质量的空间分布；2) 基于CoRM设计Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE)模块，针对不一致的特征需求提取差异化特征；3) 提出Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI)，通过双向注入细化任务感知特征，联合优化检测与BEV分割。

Result: 在OPV2V和V2V4Real数据集上的大量实验表明，CoDS在检测和BEV分割两个任务上均超越现有基线方法，并在多源噪声条件下保持稳定的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合训练检测与BEV分割并显式建模特征可靠性分布，能够有效缓解协同感知中的多源噪声问题，提升感知性能与鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Collaborative perception breaks through single-view limitations via multi-agent information exchange. However, multi-source noise such as pose errors and communication delays degrades fusion feature quality, constraining perception performance. Joint training of detection and BEV segmentation provides a natural remedy, where segmented road regions help constrain target distributions and detection bounding boxes help recover ambiguous segmentation boundaries. To this end, we propose a robust Collaborative perception framework with expert-driven Detection and bev Segmentation (CoDS). To address spatial inconsistency in fusion quality, we first introduce the Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM) to explicitly quantify feature quality distribution. Based on CoRM, we design the Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE) module to extract differentiated features for inconsistent feature demands. Finally, to further mitigate feature noise degradation, the Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI) refines task-aware features through bidirectional injection. Extensive experiments on OPV2V and V2V4Real datasets show that our CoDS surpasses existing baselines on both tasks and maintains stable robustness under multi-source noise. Code: https://github.com/JinlongW128/CoDS and https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenAIDriving/CoDS.

</details>


### [37] [Fixed-Budget Gaussian Volume Encoding with Structure-Aware Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14112)
*Michael R. Martin,Joseph Insley,Victor A. Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用各向异性高斯基元在固定预算下对标量场进行编码的方法，该方法从局部场结构中解析分配全部基元（含位置、方向和形状），然后直接对标量场进行细化，无需增密、剪枝或改变数量。截断感知的场求值可将编码时间缩短最多51倍；14百万个高斯基元可在桌面GPU上四分钟内编码十亿体素体积，缩减迭代的细化在一分钟内完成。五个数据集（210万至11亿体素）上，压缩比从2.2倍到40000倍以上，PSNR达15.0-38.7 dB。由于基元保留标量属性而非烘焙外观，单一压缩模型即可支持所有后续可视化状态（传递函数、颜色映射、光照、视角）的变换而无需重新编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟产生的标量体数据生成速度远超存储、传输和加载能力，而原位缩减只能使用有限的模拟资源。现有方法需要在这些约束下实现高效、高保真的数据压缩，同时要支持后续灵活的可视化操作。

Method: 将标量场编码为各向异性高斯基元，在固定预算下从局部场结构解析分配完整的基元集（包括位置、方向和形状），然后直接对标量场进行细化优化。使用截断感知的场求值加速编码，缩减迭代以控制细化时间预算。

Result: 截断感知场求值降低编码时间最多51倍；14百万高斯编码十亿体素在桌面GPU上最多四分钟，缩减迭代细化在一分钟内完成。五个数据集上达到15.0-38.7 dB PSNR，压缩比2.2倍到40000倍以上。预编码结构统计可表征一次性分配收效有限的场。

Conclusion: 该方法的基元保留标量属性而非烘焙外观，单一紧凑模型即可服务于后续所有可视化状态（传递函数、颜色映射、光照、视角）的调整而无需重新编码，实现了高效原位缩减与灵活后可视化之间的统一。

Abstract: Scientific simulations often produce scalar volumes faster than they can be stored, transferred, and loaded, while in situ reduction must use only a limited share of simulation resources. This work encodes scalar fields as anisotropic Gaussian primitives under a fixed budget. The complete primitive set is allocated analytically from local field structure, including position, orientation, and shape, then refined directly against the scalar field without densification, pruning, or count changes. The selected budget determines encoded storage before refinement and, together with the iteration schedule, provides a controllable refinement-time budget. In a controlled benchmark, truncation-aware field evaluation reduces encoding time by up to 51x; 1.4 million Gaussians encode a billion-voxel volume in at most four minutes on one desktop GPU, with reduced-iteration refinement completing in under one minute. Across five datasets spanning 2.1 million to 1.1 billion evaluated voxels, compression-useful configurations achieve 15.0-38.7 dB PSNR at compression ratios from 2.2x to over 40,000x. Pre-encoding structure statistics characterize fields for which one-shot allocation yields limited gains from additional capacity. Because primitives retain scalar attributes rather than baked appearance, a single compact model serves every subsequent visualization state - supporting post-hoc transfer-function, colormap, lighting, and viewpoint changes without re-encoding.

</details>


### [38] [HiCo-GS: Hierarchical Context Aggregation and Geometric Consistency for Octree Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14136)
*Wei Zhang,Shengkai Yu,Shiqiang Gong,Qi Zhang,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiCo-GS提出了一种基于八叉树锚定高斯泼溅的高保真城市场景重建框架，通过跨层级上下文聚合（CLCA）模块打破层级间特征隔离，并引入深度-法线几何一致性（DNGC）正则化抑制漂浮伪影，同时发布了包含8座中国古代宝塔的China-Pagoda数据集，在多个基准上达到SOTA渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于八叉树的锚定高斯泼溅方法存在跨层级特征隔离问题：每个层级的锚点特征独立优化，缺乏层级间通信，导致建筑立面颜色漂移和纹理区域过度平滑，限制了城市场景渲染的真实感。

Method: 提出两个互补模块：(1) 跨层级上下文聚合（CLCA）：利用八叉树空间包含结构，将逐层上下文向量聚合为父-自-子三元组，通过带残差连接的轻量MLP融合，实现双向层级先验注入——粗粒度结构先验向下传递给细粒度锚点，细粒度细节统计向上反馈防止过度平滑；(2) 深度-法线几何一致性（DNGC）正则化：通过alpha加权一致性损失强制渲染法线与深度导出法线一致，并结合渐进预热（progressive warmup）的边缘感知平滑损失，利用城市几何中普遍存在的平面先验抑制漂浮伪影。

Result: 在Mill19、UrbanScene3D、MatrixCity和China-Pagoda四个数据集上的大量实验表明，HiCo-GS在真实和合成城市场景基准上均达到最先进的渲染质量，并显著提升了几何清晰度。

Conclusion: HiCo-GS通过跨层级特征通信和几何一致性正则化，有效解决了八叉树锚定高斯泼溅的特征隔离问题，为城市场景新视角合成提供了高保真解决方案；同时发布的China-Pagoda数据集（包含8座中国古代宝塔，每座超过1200张图像，涵盖密集装饰雕刻、弯曲多层屋檐和重复细粒度纹理）为相关研究提供了具有挑战性的新基准。

Abstract: Octree-based anchor Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a scalable representation for city-scale novel view synthesis, where multi-level anchors adaptively capture scene content from coarse building structures to fine architectural details. However, we identify a fundamental limitation in existing methods: cross-level feature isolation, where each level's anchor features are optimized independently with no inter-level communication, causing color drift on building facades and over-smoothing in textured regions. We present HiCo-GS, a high-fidelity reconstruction framework with two complementary modules. Cross-Level Context Aggregation (CLCA) enables bidirectional hierarchical prior injection by leveraging the octree's spatial containment structure to aggregate per-level context vectors into parent-self-child triplets, fused via a lightweight MLP with residual connection. Coarse-level structural priors flow down to inform fine-level anchors, while fine-level detail statistics feed back to prevent over-smoothing, at negligible computational overhead. Depth-Normal Geometric Consistency (DNGC) regularization enforces agreement between rendered normals and depth-derived normals through an alpha-weighted consistency loss, complemented by edge-aware smoothness losses with progressive warmup that exploit the strong planar priors ubiquitous in urban geometry to suppress floating artifacts. We further introduce the China-Pagoda dataset comprising 8 ancient Chinese pagodas with over 1,200 images each, featuring dense ornamental carvings, curved multi-layer eaves, and repetitive fine-grained textures. Extensive experiments on Mill19, UrbanScene3D, MatrixCity, and China-Pagoda demonstrate that HiCo-GS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and substantially cleaner geometry across real-world and synthetic urban benchmarks.Code: https://github.com/WZ-CS/HiCo-GS.

</details>


### [39] [SPARGen: Unifying Spatial Perception and Reasoning through Native Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14138)
*Jinsheng Quan,Jianhua Li,Siyi Xie,Xuanke Shi,Kewang Deng,Zukai Chen,Feifei Shao,Lei Yang,Quan Wang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARGen是一个统一的多模态框架，将3D重建、密集对应和空间推理这三种互补的空间感知任务统一为指令条件生成任务，在单一原生多模态生成模型中实现联合学习与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常使用任务特定架构或外部几何模块分别处理不同空间感知任务（3D重建、对应关系、空间推理），限制了同一物理场景互补表征之间的知识迁移，缺乏统一框架来共享这些任务间的空间监督信号。

Method: SPARGen将3D重建、密集对应和空间推理建模为指令条件生成任务。它将紧凑的结构化和语言输出序列化为token序列，同时生成与图像对齐的稠密几何场，使得空间监督信号能够在原生多模态生成模型内共同塑造共享的表征。

Result: 在3D重建、对应关系及空间推理基准上的实验表明，SPARGen在单一原生多模态生成框架内，跨异构空间任务取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SPARGen证明了将多种空间感知任务统一为生成式任务，可以在单一多模态生成模型中实现高效的联合学习和知识共享，为空间理解提供更统一的解决范式。

Abstract: Spatial perception and reasoning from visual observations require recovering geometric structure, establishing correspondences, and understanding spatial relations. Existing approaches typically address these capabilities separately using task-specific architectures or external geometric modules, limiting knowledge transfer among complementary representations of the same physical scene. We introduce SPARGen, a unified multimodal framework that casts 3D reconstruction, dense correspondence, and spatial reasoning as instruction-conditioned generation tasks. SPARGen serializes compact structured and linguistic outputs as token sequences while generating dense geometric fields in image-aligned forms, enabling spatial supervision to jointly shape shared representations within a native multimodal generative model. Experiments across benchmarks for 3D reconstruction, correspondence, and spatial reasoning show that SPARGen achieves competitive performance across heterogeneous spatial tasks within a single native multimodal generative framework.

</details>


### [40] [PISA: A Pseudo-Individual Source-Domain Feature Adaptation Framework for Test-Time Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14142)
*Ziyan He,Xiongtai Yang,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PISA是一种新型的无源开放词汇目标检测测试时自适应（OVOD-TTA）方法，通过抗损坏不变特征提取器（CIFE）、特征对齐模块（FAM）和多尺度对齐框架（BAA）三大组件，将CLIP视觉特征的损坏不变性转化为接近原始源域特征的稠密伪个体特征，用于监督而非不可靠的伪标签。在VOC-C、COCO-C和LVIS-C基准上超越现有方法，COCO-C上AP@50%提升3.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源自由OVOD-TTA方法存在两个主要问题：一是依赖测试时信息重打分或伪标签自训练，在初始预测不佳时精度显著下降；二是一般源域估计方法恢复的是适合分类的抽象稀疏特征，无法捕获目标检测所需的稠密具体特征。因此需要一种能在无源数据条件下获取检测适用特征的适应方法。

Method: PISA包含三个核心组件：1) CIFE（Corruption-Invariant Feature Extractor）利用CLIP视觉特征在不同损坏图像间的不变性来提取鲁棒的抗损坏特征；2) FAM（Feature Alignment Module）用于预训练阶段，将抗损坏不变特征与源域特征对齐；3) BAA（Multi-scale Alignment Framework）用于适应阶段的多尺度对齐，将抗损坏不变特征转换为接近原始源域的伪个体特征。这些稠密具体的伪个体特征替代不可靠的伪标签进行监督。

Result: 在公开的VOC-C、COCO-C和LVIS-C基准上，跨越三个基础模型进行实验验证。PISA显著提升了原模型的定位精度和类别识别准确率。在不需要任何源域数据的情况下，PISA在COCO-C上AP@50%超越现有方法3.92%，达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: PISA证明了利用CLIP视觉特征的损坏不变性可以构建检测适用的稠密伪个体源域特征，从而有效解决无源OVOD-TTA中因伪标签不可靠和源域特征稀疏导致的精度退化问题，无需访问源域数据即可实现最先进的自适应性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection test-time adaptation (OVOD-TTA) aims to address the performance degradation that pre-trained base models suffer when encountering image-domain shifts. Existing source-free OVOD-TTA methods rely either on refined test-time information for re-scoring or on pseudo-labels for self-training, leading to significant accuracy degradation when initial predictions are poor. Meanwhile, most conventional source-domain estimation methods recover abstract, sparse representations suitable for the classification task, but fail to capture the dense, concrete features required for detection. To address these issues, we propose PISA, a novel source-free OVOD-TTA method that can be seamlessly integrated into open-vocabulary visual backbones. The core components of our method are the Corruption-Invariant Feature Extractor (CIFE), the Feature Alignment Module (FAM), and a multi-scale alignment framework (BAA). To capture detection-suitable features, we develop CIFE to exploit the invariance of CLIP's visual features across corrupted images, ensuring robustness against various corruptions. We further develop FAM and BAA for the pre-training and adaptation to transform the corruption-invariant features into pseudo-individual source-domain features that are close to the original source-domain features. In this way, dense and concrete pseudo-individual source-domain features are used for supervision instead of unreliable pseudo-label signals. Experiments on the corrupted VOC-C, COCO-C, and LVIS-C benchmarks across three base models demonstrate that PISA substantially improves both the localization precision and the category recognition accuracy of the original models. Notably, PISA achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring access to source-domain data, surpassing existing methods by 3.92% in AP@50% on COCO-C.

</details>


### [41] [Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14144)
*Yijiang Li,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Bingyang Wang,Ke Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Di Fu,Philip Torr,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督视觉在线策略蒸馏（S²VOPD）方法，通过从学生端减去信息而非向教师端添加特权信息，构建非对称增强视图生成在线学习信号。在六个细粒度感知基准上，将Qwen3.5-4B从70.7%提升至77.4%，超过所有对比的开源模型（最高达Qwen3-VL 235B）和GPT-5.4，同时恢复了特权信息方法96%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉在线策略蒸馏依赖于教师-学生之间的信息不对称，这种不对称通常来自更强的教师模型或特权监督（如参考答案、真值区域标注）。作者提出一个根本性问题：当没有任何特权信息可用时，信息不对称从何而来？这构成了研究的核心动机。

Method: S²VOPD的核心思想是反转不对称的来源方向——不从教师端添加特权信息，而是从学生端减去信息。具体做法是：将教师分布（基于原始图像）在在线策略上蒸馏到学生分布（基于同一图像的强增强视图）。作者系统探索了视觉增强的设计空间，发现：1）不对称性至关重要（四类增强族均提升性能，对称自蒸馏则退化）；2）增强强度存在最优值（中等强度性能最佳）；3）任务一致性不可或缺（完全移除问题相关证据的增强会产生大但无信息的差异）。

Result: 在六个细粒度感知基准上，S²VOPD将Qwen3.5-4B从70.7%提升至77.4%，超过所有对比的开源模型（包括高达235B参数的Qwen3-VL）以及GPT-5.4。在保持训练数据不变的情况下，恢复了使用特权信息方法所获得改进的96%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了即使在没有特权信息的条件下，也可以通过从学生端减去信息而非向教师端添加信息来创造有效的在线学习信号。这项工作重新定义了视觉策略蒸馏中信息不对称的来源，为无监督条件下的大模型视觉感知训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation relies heavily on an informative teacher-student asymmetry, through either a larger, stronger teacher or privileged supervision, such as reference answers or ground-truth regions of interest. This raises a fundamental question: where can informative asymmetry come from when nothing privileged is available? We answer this by inverting where the asymmetry comes from. Rather than adding privileged information to the teacher, we subtract information from the student. This asymmetry creates the same effective learning signal for free as a teacher with access to information unavailable to the student, without ground-truth annotations, rewards, or a separate stronger teacher model. Building on this principle, we introduce Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation (S$^2$VOPD), a simple yet effective method that constructs on-policy learning signals from asymmetric augmented views. S$^2$VOPD distills the teacher's distribution conditioned on the original image on-policy into the student distribution conditioned on a strongly augmented view of the same image. We systematically explore a broad design space of visual augmentations and uncover that (1) asymmetry matters: all four augmentation families improve performance, while symmetric self-distillation degrades it; (2) strength matters: performance peaks at a moderate strength; and (3) the gap must remain task-consistent: augmentations that completely remove the question-relevant evidence can induce large but uninformative discrepancies. Across six fine-grained perception benchmarks, S$^2$VOPD improves Qwen3.5-4B from 70.7% to 77.4%, above all open-source models compared, up to Qwen3-VL at 235B, and surpasses GPT-5.4. While holding training data the same, it recovers 96% of the improvement achieved by methods with privileged information. Website is at https://williamium3000.github.io/s2vopd

</details>


### [42] [CSG-Mamba: A Convolutional Scoring Gating Vision State Space Network for Endoscopic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14146)
*Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu,Jiaye Song,Shuxia Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSG-Mamba，一种基于卷积评分门控视觉状态空间网络的内镜息肉分割方法。该方法在VM-UNet风格的非对称U型编码器-解码器架构基础上，在语义丰富的瓶颈处插入卷积评分门控（CSG）模块，通过逐点卷积和大核深度卷积生成局部空间评分图，并用乘法门控重新校准状态空间特征，有效缓解了Vision Mamba一维扫描导致的局部几何连续性弱化和不规则轮廓过度平滑问题，在Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ColonDB数据集上取得了优于基线的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜图像普遍存在低对比度边界、黏膜纹理干扰、镜面高光和设备差异等挑战；现有Vision Mamba分割模型将2D特征转为1D扫描序列，可能削弱局部几何连续性并过度平滑不规则轮廓，需要更精细的局部空间建模方法来提升息肉分割精度。

Method: 构建VM-UNet风格的非对称U型编码器-解码器作为主干，在语义丰富的瓶颈层插入卷积评分门控（CSG）模块。CSG通过逐点卷积与大核深度卷积生成局部空间评分图，利用乘法门控机制对状态空间特征进行重新校准，在不破坏SSM长程建模优势的前提下增强局部几何信息的保留。

Result: 使用三个随机种子的实验表明，CSG-Mamba在Kvasir-SEG上达到0.9220 Dice和15.87 HD95，在CVC-ColonDB上达到0.7418 Dice和0.6570 mIoU，在大多数重叠度和召回率指标上优于基线方法，同时保持有竞争力的边界精度。

Conclusion: CSG-Mamba通过引入卷积评分门控机制，有效弥补了Vision Mamba在局部几何连续性方面的不足，在保持SSM长程建模效率的同时显著提升了息肉分割的准确性和边界质量，为内镜计算机辅助诊断提供了一种性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation is critical for computer-aided colonoscopy, yet endoscopic images often contain low-contrast boundaries, mucosal texture interference, specular highlights, and device-dependent appearance shifts. Vision State Space Models (SSMs) provide efficient long-range modeling with linear complexity, but existing Vision Mamba segmentation models typically convert 2D features into 1D scanning sequences, which may weaken local geometric continuity and over-smooth irregular contours. We propose CSG-Mamba, a convolutional scoring gating Vision State Space network for endoscopic polyp segmentation. Built on a VM-UNet-style asymmetric U-shaped encoder-decoder, CSG-Mamba inserts a Convolutional Scoring Gating (CSG) module at the semantically rich bottleneck. CSG generates a local spatial score map through pointwise and large-kernel depthwise convolutions and recalibrates state-space features by multiplicative gating. Experiments with three random seeds show that CSG-Mamba achieves 0.9220 Dice and 15.87 HD95 on Kvasir-SEG, and 0.7418 Dice and 0.6570 mIoU on CVC-ColonDB, outperforming the baselines on most overlap and recall metrics while maintaining competitive boundary accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [SCVIB: Editable State-Conditioned Visual Instance Binding forMulti-Turn Personalized Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14148)
*Xiongtai Yang,Ziyan He,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多轮定位设定——可编辑状态条件下的视觉实例绑定（SCVIB），并构建了包含1050个手工验证的支撑-查询基对和1500个episodes的基准数据集。针对直接推理仅达60.13% Joint@0.5的问题，作者提出TT-VG方法，结合目标状态转换树（TSTT）和视觉证据接地适应（VEGA），将性能提升至70.27% Joint@0.5，在匹配目标解析条件下VEGA比最强对比方法高出16.20点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮个性化定位中，现有方法未能有效利用已解析的支撑证据进行查询侧定位。直接Seq-free推理虽然能够正确解析最终引用，但并未确保有效使用相应的视觉证据，说明"解析正确引用"与"有效利用视觉证据"之间存在显著鸿沟。作者旨在建立一个受控测试平台来研究这一核心挑战。

Method: 作者提出TT-VG（Transition-Tree Visual Grounding）方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) TSTT（Target-State Transition Tree）将可见交互编译为协议定义的事件，在版本化目标状态上执行，并将最终查询引用解析到对应的支撑证据；(2) VEGA（Visual Evidence Grounding Adaptation）在轨迹派生的同实例对上适应，使用视觉证据包对已解析实例进行支撑条件接地。训练数据来自轨迹构造的同实例对。

Result: 直接Seq-free推理仅达60.13% Joint@0.5；TT-VG达到70.27% Joint@0.5；在匹配目标解析条件下，VEGA比最强对比方法高出16.20点。在Counter-Recency和Rollback（需要路由到非最新或恢复的支撑证据的任务）上，相比直接推理的增益最大。

Conclusion: SCVIB可作为受控测试平台用于评估多轮个性化定位；有效使用已解析的支撑证据进行查询侧同实例定位是该领域的一个核心挑战。TT-VG通过显式的转换树推理和视觉证据适应有效弥合了引用解析与证据利用之间的差距。

Abstract: We introduce editable state-conditioned visual instance binding, a multi-turn localization setting in which several support-defined instances are introduced across turns and protocol-defined state events determine the final target. We instantiate this setting as SCVIB, comprising 1,050 manually verified support--query base pairs and 1,500 episodes spanning five visual domains, three difficulty levels, and four target-state dependency groups. Direct Seq-free inference reaches only 60.13\% Joint@0.5, indicating that resolving the final reference does not ensure effective use of the corresponding visual evidence for query-side localization. We address this gap with TT-VG (Transition-Tree Visual Grounding), which combines a Target-State Transition Tree (TSTT) with Visual Evidence Grounding Adaptation (VEGA). TSTT compiles the visible interaction into protocol-defined events, executes them over versioned target states, and resolves the final-query reference to the corresponding support evidence. Adapted on trajectory-derived same-instance pairs, VEGA performs support-conditioned grounding of the resolved instance using a Visual Evidence Package. TT-VG reaches 70.27\% Joint@0.5; under matched target resolution, VEGA exceeds the strongest comparison method by 16.20 points. Gains over direct inference are largest on Counter-Recency and Rollback, which require routing to non-latest or restored support evidence. Together, these results establish SCVIB as a controlled testbed and highlight the effective use of resolved support evidence for query-side same-instance localization as a central challenge in multi-turn personalized localization.

</details>


### [44] [Concept Guidance: Precise, Training-Free Latent Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14172)
*Nikolai Röhrich,Isabell Hans,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出概念引导（CoG）方法，通过引入概念级互信息的概念，发现文本到图像扩散模型中不同层对特定概念的生成有局部化影响，并通过强化概念相关层、跳过无关层来生成加权组合预测，实现对图像美学、文字、手部等目标的精确、连续引导，无需额外训练、外部模型或梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型存在两大缺陷：(1) 缺乏用于连续、概念特定的引导机制（如精确控制图像美观程度）；(2) 在需要高局部一致性的任务（如生成文字或人手）上可靠性差，严重影响实际应用。

Method: 提出概念级互信息的新概念，揭示不同层的概念相关性差异，证明特定结构的生成定位在网络不同部分。基于此提出CoG方法：先量化每层的概念特定影响，然后通过跳过概念相关层的预测进行加权组合来引导去噪过程。该方法无需额外训练、外部模型、梯度或提示工程，可直接适用于现有模型。

Result: 在PixArt-alpha、SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.1-dev等多个主流模型上，针对不同目标均展示出性能提升。

Conclusion: CoG通过识别并利用网络层与概念之间的局部化对应关系，实现了精确、目标特定的引导，以零训练即插即用的方式显著改善了扩散模型在概念控制和局部一致性方面的能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have two major drawbacks that severely limit their practical utility: (1) standard models lack an intrinsic mechanism for continuous, concept-specific guidance (e.g., for precisely controlling how aesthetically pleasing an image looks), and (2) they lack reliability for tasks requiring high local coherence (e.g., generating text or human hands). To tackle these issues, we introduce a novel notion of concept-wise mutual information and find large, concept-dependent differences between individual layers, demonstrating that the generation of specific structures is localized in distinct parts of the network. We exploit this insight by reinforcing the impact of concept-relevant layers in Concept Guidance (CoG), a precise, target-specific guidance method that works for models out-of-the-box without additional training, external models, gradients, or prompt engineering. CoG first quantifies each layer's concept-specific impact and then guides denoising using a weighted combination of predictions generated with concept-relevant layers skipped. We demonstrate performance increases across various targets and popular models like PixArt-alpha, SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.1-dev. Code is available at https://github.com/CompVis/concept_guidance

</details>


### [45] [LightTeaNet: A Weakly Supervised Lightweight CNN for Multi-Label Tea Leaf Disease Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14178)
*Naif Haider Chowdhury,Md Rahim,Syed Farhan Hasan,Murad Hasan,Prithwiraj Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LightTeaNet，一种基于弱监督多标签分类与疾病定位的轻量级卷积神经网络，用于茶叶病害检测。它仅使用图像级标签训练，通过类激活映射（CAM）自动定位病害区域，无需如YOLO那样的边界框标注。该网络结合深度可分离卷积与通道注意力机制，在无手动标注的情况下达到Precision 0.9615、Recall 0.8772、F1-score 0.9179，mAP@0.50为0.1810，验证了其作为可解释且资源高效的农业智能病害监测框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 茶叶是南亚和东南亚重要的经济作物，但其产量和质量长期受多种病害影响。传统人工巡检方法存在不一致性、劳动密集且依赖大量人工监测等问题，亟需一种自动化、高效且资源友好的病害检测方案。

Method: 提出LightTeaNet轻量级CNN架构：1）采用弱监督学习范式，仅需图像级标签即可训练，避免了传统目标检测（如YOLO）所需的昂贵边界框标注；2）利用类激活映射（CAM）自动定位病害区域；3）集成深度可分离卷积提升参数效率；4）引入通道注意力机制增强特征判别能力。

Result: LightTeaNet在无任何手动标注条件下实现了Precision 0.9615、Recall 0.8772和F1-score 0.9179，同时在弱监督定位任务上mAP@0.50达到0.1810，展现出具有竞争力的定位性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了LightTeaNet作为可解释且资源高效的智能病害监测框架的可行性，为农业领域的自动化病害监控提供了新的技术路径，兼顾了模型部署的低资源需求和检测结果的可解释性。

Abstract: Tea is known as an important crop in many parts of South and Southeast Asia, yet the production of tea is still hampered by the multiple diseases that decrease the quantity and quality. Traditional methods of inspection, which are manual, are not consistent, labor-intensive, and depend on extensive monitoring. This paper introduces a lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for weakly supervised multi-label classification and disease localization in tea leaves called LightTeaNet. LightTeaNet learns directly from image-level labels and employs Class Activation Mapping (CAM) to localize disease-affected regions automatically, unlike conventional object detection models such as YOLO, which require extensive bounding box annotations. For Parameter efficiency, the network integrates Depthwise Separable Convolutions, and for enhanced feature discrimination, it integrates Channel Attention. LightTeaNet has achieved a Precision of 0.9615, a Recall of 0.8772, and an F1-score of 0.9179, while it shows mAP@0.50=0.1810 without any manual annotations, which delivers a competitive localization performance in the experimental results. These results validate the model as an interpretable as well as a resource-efficient framework for intelligent disease monitoring in agriculture.

</details>


### [46] [RankT2I: A Submodular Framework for Discovering Interpretable and Diverse Semantics in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14226)
*Ritika Allada,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RankT2I是一个无需训练、模型无关的框架，用于自动发现文本到图像（T2I）编辑模型（包括扩散模型和FLUX模型）中可编辑的语义。该方法先利用多模态视觉语言模型收集候选语义，再将语义发现建模为集合选择问题，通过子模目标函数识别相关、可编辑且多样化的语义，无需用户手动指定编辑内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型虽在图像生成和编辑方面取得了突破性进展，但识别模型能够成功编辑的语义仍然困难。大多数现有方法需要用户手动指定要修改的语义，这是一个耗时且常需反复试错的过程。因此需要一种自动化的方法来发现可编辑的语义。

Method: RankT2I采用三步流程：(1) 利用多模态视觉语言模型(VLM)在给定视觉域中收集广泛的候选语义；(2) 将语义发现形式化为集合选择问题；(3) 使用子模目标函数（submodular objective）选择相关、可编辑且多样化的语义子集。该框架无需训练且模型无关，适用于扩散模型和FLUX模型。

Result: RankT2I在多个视觉域中帮助用户高效识别广泛的编辑语义，并在性能上超越了现有方法。

Conclusion: RankT2I通过自动化语义发现流程，显著降低了用户在T2I编辑中使用模型的门槛，提供了一种高效、可扩展且模型无关的解决方案，有望推动文本到图像编辑在更多实际场景中的应用。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) models have revolutionized the field of image generation and editing. However, identifying semantics that a T2I model can successfully edit in an image continues to be a challenging task. Most existing approaches require users to manually specify semantics to modify a particular image, a time-consuming process that often involves extensive trial and error. In this paper, we present RankT2I, a novel, training-free, and model-agnostic framework that automates the discovery of editable semantics in diffusion and FLUX-based models. Given a visual domain, we first utilize a multimodal vision-language model to gather a broad set of candidate semantics. We then frame semantic discovery as a set selection problem and use a submodular objective to identify semantics that are relevant, editable, and diverse. Our method helps users efficiently identify a wide range of semantics for text-to-image editing models across several domains while outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [47] [AppleScab-LT: A Longitudinal Real-Field Apple Scab Dataset for Temporal Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14235)
*Aamir Hilal,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Neeraj Goel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AppleScab-LT，一个首个针对苹果黑星病纵向监测的真实田间数据集，包含21个纵向叶片序列、2,101张高分辨率图像和264个渐进时间样本，支持病害随时间演变和严重程度进展的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的植物病害数据集大多为单一时点的静态图像，无法捕捉病害在自然田间条件下的时间演化和严重程度进展，限制了可靠的病害监测系统开发。

Method: 采用研究问题驱动的框架，通过系统化果园监测、纵向叶片追踪、专家验证、多边形标注、叶片隔离、病害严重度量化和时间序列构建来建立数据集，并应用包含标准化注释协议、专家验证、自动化完整性检查、序列级验证和时间一致性分析的综合质量保证框架。

Result: 数据集包含21个纵向叶片序列、2,101张高分辨率图像和264个渐进时间样本，捕获了严重程度累积、进展速率、监测持续时间和叶片间进展的变异性，并提供了基于像素严重度、颜色强度严重度和归一化相对严重度的定量病害描述符。

Conclusion: AppleScab-LT为时间性病害智能、病害进展建模、精准农业和未来作物健康监测提供了可靠的数据资源。

Abstract: The development of reliable plant disease monitoring systems is constrained by limited longitudinal datasets capturing disease progression under natural field conditions. Although existing plant disease datasets have advanced image-based recognition, most consist of static images acquired at a single time point, limiting analysis of temporal disease evolution and severity progression. To address this gap, this study presents AppleScab-LT, a longitudinal real-field dataset developed to monitor apple scab progression through repeated observations of individually tracked infected leaves. Guided by a research-question-driven framework, the dataset was systematically developed, validated, and characterized for reliable longitudinal disease analysis. AppleScab-LT was constructed through systematic orchard monitoring under natural environmental conditions, incorporating longitudinal leaf tracking, expert-guided disease verification, polygon-based annotation, leaf isolation, disease severity quantification, and temporal sequence construction. A comprehensive quality assurance framework, including standardized annotation protocols, expert validation, automated integrity checks, sequence-level verification, and temporal consistency analysis, was applied throughout curation. The dataset contains 21 longitudinal leaf sequences, 2,101 high-resolution images, and 264 progressive temporal samples from repeated monitoring of same infected leaves. It captures variability in severity accumulation, progression rates, monitoring duration, and inter-leaf progression. Quantitative disease descriptors based on pixel severity, color-intensity severity, and normalized relative severity provide standardized measurements for temporal disease analysis. AppleScab-LT provides a reliable resource for temporal disease intelligence, disease progression modelling, precision agriculture, and future crop health monitoring

</details>


### [48] [Zero-Shot Skeleton-Based Action Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14243)
*Hongsong Wang,Pengbo Yan,Yang Zhang,Qiuxia Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本骨架动作预期（ZS-SkAA）新任务，要求仅利用有限的早期骨架序列识别未见过的动作类别，并构建了包含时空特征提取器和互信息估计最大化模块的基线模型，在NTU RGB+D数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架动作预期方法假设所有动作类别在训练时可见，限制了其在真实场景中面对新动作类别的部署能力。为解决这一局限，作者提出并研究零样本骨架动作预期任务，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 构建了ZS-SkAA基线模型，包含两个核心组件：(1) 时空特征提取器，用于从部分观测的骨架序列中提取特征；(2) 互信息估计与最大化模块，通过估计并最大化部分视觉特征与语义类别嵌入之间的互信息，实现跨模态对齐，从而增强对未见类别的泛化能力。同时基于NTU RGB+D数据集设计了专用的基准评测协议。

Result: 实验表明该基线模型在NTU RGB+D数据集上取得了较高的零样本准确率，作为ZS-SkAA的强基线具有有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了ZS-SkAA作为面向真实世界系统中需要泛化到新动作类别场景的重要研究方向，为后续研究提供了基准模型和评测协议。

Abstract: Action anticipation (AA) aims to recognize ongoing human or humanoids actions from partial observations, enabling robots to predict intentions before the actions are completed. Although skeleton-based AA offers efficiency advantages, existing approaches assume that all action classes are seen during training, which limits their deployment in real-world scenarios where novel actions inevitably arise. To address this gap, we study the new task of Zero-Shot Skeleton-Based Action Anticipation (ZS-SkAA). This task requires recognizing unseen action classes using only limited early-stage skeleton sequences, combining the challenges of partial observations, temporal dynamics, and zero-shot generalization. To establish foundational research for ZS-SkAA, we introduce:(1) A baseline model comprising a spatio-temporal feature extractor and a mutual information estimation and maximization module. This baseline model explicitly aligns partial visual features with semantic class embeddings across modalities by estimating and maximizing their mutual information, enhancing generalization to unseen classes.(2) A benchmark protocol using the NTU RGB+D dataset, which is adapted for rigorous ZS-SkAA evaluation. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model as a strong baseline for ZS-SkAA, achieving high zero-shot accuracy on NTU RGB+D. This work establishes ZS-SkAA as a vital research direction for real-world systems requiring generalization to novel actions.

</details>


### [49] [Seeing Red, Thinking Bad: Color Bias in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14286)
*Kohsuke Ide,Ryousuke Yamada,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yutaka Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）如何处理以图像形式渲染的文本，揭示视觉样式偏见（如颜色和对比度）对VLM语义理解的影响。作者引入"隐形视觉提示"（Stealth Visual Prompts）来微妙改变文本的视觉样式而不改变语义内容，实验发现将正面词汇着色为绿色会持续将情感预测推向积极方向，降低文本-背景对比度会增加对视觉显著线索的依赖并导致更多VQA错误，表明VLM对渲染文本的视觉样式处理与人类语义理解存在偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM在工业决策系统（如招聘支持、推荐系统）中的广泛应用，理解VLM如何融合视觉和文本信息变得至关重要。现有研究较少关注VLM对图像形式文本的视觉样式偏见，而这些偏见可能导致模型在语义理解上偏离人类判断，存在实际部署中的风险和公平性问题。

Method: 作者提出Stealth Visual Prompts方法，通过微调渲染文本的颜色和对比度等视觉样式（保持语义内容不变），系统性地控制文本中单词的视觉样式，进而测量这些视觉扰动对VLM输出及视觉编码器潜在表征的影响。该方法包括情感分析任务和视觉问答（VQA）任务两个方向的实验设计，并结合对视觉编码器隐层表征的关联性分析。

Result: 实验发现：1）将正面词汇着色为绿色会一致地将情感预测推向积极方向，导致VLM常无法正确考虑文本中的负面词汇；2）该行为与视觉编码器潜在表征因颜色变化而产生的改变相关；3）降低文本-背景对比度会增加对视觉显著线索的依赖，导致更多不正确的VQA输出。

Conclusion: 渲染文本的视觉样式可以引导VLM的解读方式，使其偏离人类的语义理解。这一发现对VLM在工业决策场景中的可靠性提出了警示，强调了视觉样式偏见需要被纳入VLM安全性和公平性评估的考量范围。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in industrial decision-making systems, such as recruitment support and recommendation. This motivates careful analysis of how VLMs process visual and textual information. In this work, we study how VLMs interpret text rendered as an image, and investigate the influence of visual styling biases. To this end, we introduce Stealth Visual Prompts, which subtly change visual styling of text, such as color and contrast, while preserving semantic content. Using these prompts, we systematically control the visual styling of words in text and measure their impact on the analysis performed by VLMs. We further analyze how such visual perturbations affect the latent representations of the vision encoder. From our experiments, we observed that coloring positive words in green consistently shifts sentiment predictions toward a positive direction. As a result, VLMs often fail to properly account for negative words present in the text. Our analysis suggests that this behavior is correlated with changes in the latent representations of the vision encoder induced by color variations. In addition, we show that reducing text--background contrast increases reliance on visually salient cues and leads to more incorrect Visual Question Answering (VQA) outputs. These results suggest that the visual styling of rendered text can guide VLMs' interpretation in ways that diverge from human semantic understanding.
  Project page: https://github.com/KohsukeIde/color-bias-vlm

</details>


### [50] [On the Robustness of Temporal Vision-Language Models for Surgical Endoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14262)
*Darakshan Rashid,Raza Imam,Ufaq Khan,Muhammad Bilal,Shazad Ashraf,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Mohammad Yaqub,Muhammad Haris Khan,Imran Razzak,Brejesh Lall,Lena Maier-Hein,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究时序视觉语言模型（TVLMs）在真实临床内窥镜场景中对视频退化干扰的鲁棒性。作者引入Endo-C6基准，包含六种内窥镜特异性扰动，在胃肠镜和腹腔镜胆囊切除视频上系统评估3个基线模型（共294项数据集级评估），并提出基于VeRA轻量级参数高效微调的RobustEndoCLIP，在保持提示接口不变的同时显著提升退化鲁棒性。研究发现现成TVLMs在特定内窥镜退化下会出现严重的"最坏情况崩溃"，而轻量few-shot适配可大幅改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床内窥镜视频中普遍存在散焦、雾化、运动模糊、噪声、电灼烟雾和包丢失等采集伪影，这些结构化的分布偏移会破坏视频-文本对齐，导致时序视觉语言模型在真实临床场景中表现不可靠。然而，现有研究对TVLMs在这类真实退化条件下的鲁棒性缺乏系统性刻画，阻碍了临床视觉语言系统的可靠性评估与部署。

Method: (1) 构建Endo-C6基准：定义六种内窥镜真实扰动（散焦、雾化、运动模糊、噪声、电灼烟雾、包丢失），在固定高严重度下评估，应用于胃肠内镜和腹腔镜胆囊切除术视频； (2) 采用标准化提示协议，在均值与最坏情况两种设定下系统评估3个近期手术TVLM基线模型，覆盖294项数据集级评估； (3) 提出RobustEndoCLIP，利用VeRA（向量低秩适配）进行few-shot参数高效微调。

Result: 作者展示了294项数据集级评估结果，发现现成TVLMs在内窥镜特异性退化下可表现出严重的"最坏情况折叠"（worst-case collapse），而通过轻量级few-shot适配（RobustEndoCLIP）可在不改变提示接口的前提下显著提升退化条件下的性能与鲁棒性，超越现有TVLM基线。

Conclusion: Endo-C6可作为标准化鲁棒性评测基准，支持更可靠的临床视觉语言系统开发。轻量级参数高效适配是提升TVLMs在真实临床环境下退化鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Temporal vision-language models (TVLMs) offer a reusable, prompt-based interface for surgical video understanding, yet, their robustness under clinically realistic acquisition artifacts in endoscopy remains insufficiently characterized. In practice, degradations such as defocus, haze, motion blur, noise, cautery smoke, and packet loss introduce structured distribution shifts which may compromise video-text alignment. We study the robustness of temporal VLMs under such shifts caused by corruptions in clip frames. We introduce Endo-C6, a compact corruption benchmark of six endoscopy-realistic perturbations evaluated at a fixed high severity, and apply it to public Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy and laparoscopic cholecystectomy videos. Under a standardized prompt protocol, we benchmark 3 recent surgical TVLM baselines and analyze robustness in both mean and worst-case settings, spanning 294 dataset-level evaluations. Finally, we present RobustEndoCLIP, obtained by few-shot parameter-efficient tuning with VeRA, outperforming existing TVLM baselines. Our findings show that off-the-shelf TVLMs can exhibit severe worst-case collapse under endoscopy-specific corruptions, whereas lightweight few-shot adaptation can substantially improve corrupted performance and robustness without changing the prompt-based interface. We expect Endo-C6 to support standardized robustness reporting and promote more reliable clinical vision-language systems.

</details>

*Dominik Senti,Mehmet Ozgur Turkoglu,Michele Volpi,Helge Aasen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨是否可以利用地球观测时间序列和气象数据在国家尺度上预测未来作物冠层发育，以瑞士全境的冬小麦为对象，将作物生长预测建模为预测未来叶面积指数（LAI）轨迹的任务。研究在包含超过2000万个像素级Sentinel-2衍生LAI时间序列及气象变量的多年数据集上评估，针对云层覆盖和重访间隙导致LAI监督稀疏、模型拟合后产生不合理的振荡轨迹问题，引入了轻量级的单峰形状正则化器以提升轨迹合理性且几乎不损失精度。通过对比深度学习序列到序列（Seq2Seq）模型与经典机器学习基线，结果显示Seq2Seq模型跨年泛化良好，R²超过0.8并持续优于传统方法，表明遥感和天气驱动的序列建模能够在景观尺度学习作物生长动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测农业景观中的作物生长对于提高耕作系统的生产力、韧性和运营管理至关重要，但目前尚不清楚地球观测时间序列和气象驱动因素能否在国家尺度上预测未来的冠层发育，尤其是面对云层覆盖和重访间隙造成的稀疏监督数据，现有模型的轨迹在观测点之间会不合理地振荡。

Method: 将作物生长预测形式化为预测超越最后一个可用Sentinel-2观测的未来LAI轨迹；引入轻量级单峰形状正则化器来约束轨迹的合理性；对比深度学习序列到序列（Seq2Seq）模型与经典机器学习基线方法，在覆盖瑞士全境、含超2000万像素级LAI时间序列和气象变量的多年数据集上进行评估。

Result: Seq2Seq模型在跨年泛化上表现优异，R²超过0.8，持续优于经典机器学习基线；单峰形状正则化器显著提升了轨迹合理性，且仅以极小精度损失为代价。

Conclusion: 研究证明遥感与天气驱动的序列建模能够在景观尺度学习作物生长动态，为作物生长预测在方法上提供了可行且可扩展的途径，并展示了形状先验在稀疏时空监督下的正则化价值。

Abstract: Forecasting crop growth across agricultural landscapes is important for improving the productivity, resilience, and operational management of farming systems. In this work, we investigate whether Earth observation time series and meteorological drivers can be used to predict future canopy development at country scale. We focus on winter wheat and formulate crop growth prediction as forecasting future leaf area index (LAI) trajectories beyond the last available Sentinel-2 observation. We evaluate this task on a multi-year dataset which spans the entire country of Switzerland, containing over 20 million pixel-level Sentinel-2-derived LAI time series paired with meteorological variables. Because cloud cover and revisit gaps leave LAI supervision sparse, models fit the few valid (cloud-free) LAI observations yet oscillate implausibly between them, producing trajectories no real canopy could follow. We introduce a lightweight unimodal shape regulariser which improves trajectory plausibility with negligible loss in accuracy. We compare deep learning sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) models with classic machine learning baselines and show that Seq2Seq models generalise well across years, achieving $\mathrm{R}^2$ above 0.8 and consistently outperforming conventional approaches. Together, these results demonstrate that remote sensing and weather-driven sequence modelling can learn crop growth dynamics at landscape scale. S
